-- ==========================================
--  UI SOUND HELPERS & NOTIFICATION ENGINE
-- ==========================================
local function playClickSound()
    pcall(function()
        local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
        sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://6895079853"
        sound.Volume = 0.3
        sound.Parent = SoundService
        sound:Play()
        sound.Ended:Connect(function() sound:Destroy() end)
    end)
end

local function notify(title, content, duration)
    pcall(function()
        duration = duration or 3
        local parentGui = (typeof(gethui) == "function") and gethui() or CoreGui
        local notifHolder = parentGui:FindFirstChild("ObsidianGlass_NotifHolder")
        if not notifHolder then
            notifHolder = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
            notifHolder.Name = "ObsidianGlass_NotifHolder"
            notifHolder.ResetOnSpawn = false
            notifHolder.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
            notifHolder.Parent = parentGui
        end

        local toast = Instance.new("Frame")
        toast.Size = UDim2.new(0, 280, 0, 60)
        toast.Position = UDim2.new(1, 20, 1, -80)
        toast.BackgroundColor3 = COLORS.glass
        toast.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
        toast.BorderSizePixel = 0
        toast.ZIndex = 999999
        toast.Parent = notifHolder

        local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
        corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
        corner.Parent = toast

        local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(70, 74, 80)
        stroke.Thickness = 1.5
        stroke.Parent = toast

        local tTitle = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        tTitle.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 20)
        tTitle.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 6)
        tTitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        tTitle.Text = title
        tTitle.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        tTitle.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        tTitle.TextSize = 13
        tTitle.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        tTitle.Parent = toast

        local tDesc = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        tDesc.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 28)
        tDesc.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 26)
        tDesc.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        tDesc.Text = content
        tDesc.TextColor3 = COLORS.text
        tDesc.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
        tDesc.TextSize = 11
        tDesc.TextWrapped = true
        tDesc.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        tDesc.Parent = toast

        playClickSound()
        TweenService:Create(toast, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Position = UDim2.new(1, -300, 1, -80) }):Play()
        task.delay(duration, function()
            if toast and toast.Parent then
                local tw = TweenService:Create(toast, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.In), { Position = UDim2.new(1, 20, 1, -80) })
                tw:Play()
                tw.Completed:Connect(function() toast:Destroy() end)
            end
        end)
    end)
end

-- Avatar Loader Helper
local customAvatarAsset = nil
local function loadCustomAvatarImage()
    if customAvatarAsset then return customAvatarAsset end
    local avatarUrl = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/aslamdunk7/paypmboygang/main/543199739_2812856088914181_3062917809445648175_n.jpg"
    local fileName = "NAPHUB_avatar.jpg"

    pcall(function()
        if typeof(writefile) == "function" and (typeof(getcustomasset) == "function" or typeof(getsynasset) == "function") then
            local getAsset = getcustomasset or getsynasset
            local isFileExist = (typeof(isfile) == "function" and isfile(fileName))
            if not isFileExist then
                local imageBytes = game:HttpGet(avatarUrl)
                if imageBytes and #imageBytes > 0 then
                    writefile(fileName, imageBytes)
                end
            end
            if typeof(isfile) == "function" and isfile(fileName) then
                customAvatarAsset = getAsset(fileName)
            end
        end
    end)

    if not customAvatarAsset then
        customAvatarAsset = "rbxthumb://type=AvatarHeadShot&id=" .. LocalPlayer.UserId .. "&w=150&h=150"
    end
    return customAvatarAsset
end

-- ==========================================
--  LOGOUT & KEY CLEARING & SCRIPT TERMINATION
-- ==========================================
local function performLogoutKeyClear()
    pcall(function()
        local filesToDelete = {
            "NAPHUB_LuarmorKey.txt",
            "NAPHUB_VVIPKey.txt",
            "NAPHUB_SavedKey.txt"
        }
        if LuarmorConfig and type(LuarmorConfig) == "table" and LuarmorConfig.SavedKeyFile then
            table.insert(filesToDelete, LuarmorConfig.SavedKeyFile)
        end

        local del = (type(delfile) == "function" and delfile) or (type(deletefile) == "function" and deletefile)
        for _, file in ipairs(filesToDelete) do
            pcall(function()
                if isfile and isfile(file) then
                    if del then
                        del(file)
                    elseif type(writefile) == "function" then
                        writefile(file, "")
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end)

    pcall(function()
        if getgenv then
            getgenv().script_key = nil
            getgenv().NAPHUB_InputKey = nil
            getgenv().NAPHUB_LoggedOut = true
        end
        if getrenv then pcall(function() getrenv().script_key = nil end) end
        if getfenv then pcall(function() getfenv().script_key = nil end) end
        if _G then _G.script_key = nil end
        if shared then shared.script_key = nil end
        script_key = nil
    end)
end

local WarZ = {} -- ฟังก์ชัน ลดดีด / ปั๊มยาออโต้ (ตัวจริงอยู่ท้ายเมนู)

local function stopAllScriptOperations()
    pcall(function()
        Settings.ESPEnabled = false
        Settings.TeamCheck = true
        Settings.FPSBoost = false
        Settings.HighlightESP = false
        Settings.BoxESP = false
        Settings.SkeletonESP = false
        Settings.NameESP = false
        Settings.HPESP = false
        Settings.ShowLocalPlayer = false
        Settings.AimbotEnabled = false
        Settings.ShowFOV = false
        Settings.Noclip = false
        Settings.NoRecoil = false
        Settings.AutoHeal = false
        Settings.AutoLoot = false
        Settings.AutoFish = false
        Settings.AutoFarmLoot = false
        Settings.InfiniteStamina = false
        if WarZ and WarZ.SetInfiniteStamina then pcall(function() WarZ.SetInfiniteStamina(false) end) end
        Settings.BunnyHop = false
        Settings.FastWeaponSwap = false
        if WarZ and WarZ.SetFastWeaponSwap then pcall(function() WarZ.SetFastWeaponSwap(false) end) end
        Settings.LootESP = false
        if WarZ and WarZ.SetAutoFish then pcall(function() WarZ.SetAutoFish(false) end) end
        if WarZ and WarZ.SetAutoFarmLoot then pcall(function() WarZ.SetAutoFarmLoot(false) end) end
        if WarZ and WarZ.RestoreRecoil then pcall(WarZ.RestoreRecoil) end
        for _, lbl in pairs(lootLabels or {}) do pcall(function() lbl:Destroy() end) end
        lootLabels = {}

        if toggleFPSBoost then
            pcall(function() toggleFPSBoost(false) end)
        end

        for _, line in pairs(tracers) do pcall(function() line:Destroy() end) end
        for _, label in pairs(espLabels) do pcall(function() label:Destroy() end) end
        for _, b in pairs(espBoxes) do pcall(function() b:Destroy() end) end
        tracers = {}
        espLabels = {}
        espBoxes = {}

        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if p ~= player and p.Character then
                local hl = p.Character:FindFirstChild("NAPHUBHighlight")
                if hl then pcall(function() hl:Destroy() end) end
                for _, partName in ipairs({"HumanoidRootPart", "Torso", "UpperTorso", "LowerTorso"}) do
                    local part = p.Character:FindFirstChild(partName)
                    if part and part:IsA("BasePart") and part:FindFirstChild("OrigSize") then
                        part.Size = part.OrigSize.Value
                        if part.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" then part.Transparency = 1 else part.Transparency = 0 end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    pcall(function() if _G.NapHubExtraCleanup then _G.NapHubExtraCleanup() end end)
end

-- ฟังก์ชันเกมเดิมด้านล่างคงไว้ทั้งหมด ไม่แก้ตรรกะ

if _G.NapHubCleanup then
    pcall(_G.NapHubCleanup)
    task.wait(0.1)
end

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
while not LocalPlayer do
    task.wait(0.05)
    LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
end

-- GUI container resolver
local cloneref = cloneref or function(i) return i end
local CoreGui = cloneref(game:GetService("CoreGui"))
local function getSafeGuiContainer()
    if gethui then
        local ok, h = pcall(gethui)
        if ok and typeof(h) == "Instance" then return h end
    end
    local ok = pcall(function()
        local t = Instance.new("Folder"); t.Parent = CoreGui; t:Destroy()
    end)
    if ok then return CoreGui end
    return LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 10)
end

local function safeDestroyGui(name)
    pcall(function()
        local parents = { CoreGui, LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") }
        if gethui then pcall(function() table.insert(parents, gethui()) end) end
        for _, p in ipairs(parents) do
            if p then
                for _, c in ipairs(p:GetChildren()) do
                    if c.Name == name then pcall(function() c:Destroy() end) end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end
safeDestroyGui("NapHub_LootToForge_Floating")
safeDestroyGui("NapHubUI")
safeDestroyGui("WarZ_LootESP")

_G.NapHubConns = {}
local RUN = {}            -- token ของการรันรอบนี้ (ใช้หยุด loop ของรอบเก่า)
_G.NapHubRun = RUN

local function bind(signal, fn)
    local c = signal:Connect(fn)
    table.insert(_G.NapHubConns, c)
    return c
end

_G.NapHubCleanup = function()
    _G.NapHubRun = nil
    pcall(stopAllScriptOperations)
    for _, c in ipairs(_G.NapHubConns or {}) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
    _G.NapHubConns = {}
    pcall(function() game:GetService("RunService"):UnbindFromRenderStep("NapHubAimbot") end)
    pcall(function() game:GetService("RunService"):UnbindFromRenderStep("NapHubDrawESP") end)
    if _G.NapHubDrawClear then pcall(_G.NapHubDrawClear) end
    safeDestroyGui("NapHub_LootToForge_Floating")
    safeDestroyGui("NapHubUI")
    safeDestroyGui("ESP_Tracers")
    safeDestroyGui("NAPHUBFOV")
    safeDestroyGui("WarZ_LootESP")
end

local GuiParent = getSafeGuiContainer()

-- ขอบบางสำหรับกล่องข้อมูลเสริม (ฟังก์ชันด้านล่างเรียกใช้ชื่อนี้)
local function addStroke(obj, color)
    local st = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    st.Color = color or Color3.fromRGB(74, 74, 74)
    st.Thickness = 1
    st.Transparency = 0.15
    st.Parent = obj
    return st
end

local function hex(h)
    return Color3.fromRGB(tonumber(h:sub(1, 2), 16), tonumber(h:sub(3, 4), 16), tonumber(h:sub(5, 6), 16))
end

local C = {
    Body = hex("000000"),

local ALPHABET = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/"
local LOOKUP = {}
for i = 1, #ALPHABET do LOOKUP[ALPHABET:byte(i)] = i - 1 end

local function b64decode(s)
    s = s:gsub("[^%w%+/=]", "")
    local out, n = {}, 0
    local buf, bn = {}, 0
    for i = 1, #s, 4 do
        local a, b, c, d = s:byte(i, i + 3)
        local v = (LOOKUP[a] or 0) * 262144 + (LOOKUP[b] or 0) * 4096 + (LOOKUP[c] or 0) * 64 + (LOOKUP[d] or 0)
        local b1 = math.floor(v / 65536) % 256
        local b2 = math.floor(v / 256) % 256
        local b3 = v % 256
        bn = bn + 1
        if c == 61 then
            buf[bn] = string.char(b1)
        elseif d == 61 then
            buf[bn] = string.char(b1, b2)
        else
            buf[bn] = string.char(b1, b2, b3)
        end
        if bn >= 2000 then
            n = n + 1
            out[n] = table.concat(buf, "", 1, bn)
            buf, bn = {}, 0
        end
    end
    if bn > 0 then
        n = n + 1
        out[n] = table.concat(buf, "", 1, bn)
    end
    return table.concat(out)
end

local function loadAsset(b64, path)
    if not (writefile and getcustomasset) then return nil end
    local ok, asset = pcall(function()
        if not (isfile and isfile(path)) then
            writefile(path, b64decode(b64))
        end
        return getcustomasset(path)
    end)
    if ok and asset and asset ~= "" then return asset end
    return nil
end

local function loadBackground()
    if IMAGE_ASSET_ID ~= "" then return IMAGE_ASSET_ID end
    return loadAsset(BG_B64, "NAPHUB_bg_v3.jpg")
end

local function loadWord()
    return loadAsset(WORD_B64, "NAPHUB_word_v1.jpg")
end

local function loadLogo()
    if LOGO_ASSET_ID ~= "" then return LOGO_ASSET_ID end
    return loadAsset(LOGO_B64, "NAPHUB_logo_v2.jpg")
end

    local function new(class, props, children)
        local inst = Instance.new(class)
        for k, v in pairs(props or {}) do
            inst[k] = v
        end
        for _, child in ipairs(children or {}) do
            child.Parent = inst
        end
        return inst
    end

    local function corner(r)
        return new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = type(r) == "number" and UDim.new(0, r) or r })
    end

    local function stroke(color, thickness)
        return new("UIStroke", { Color = color, Thickness = thickness or 2, ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border })
    end

    local function list(padding, horizontal, halign, valign)
        return new("UIListLayout", {
            Padding = UDim.new(0, padding or 0),
            FillDirection = horizontal and Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal or Enum.FillDirection.Vertical,
            HorizontalAlignment = halign or Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Left,
            VerticalAlignment = valign or Enum.VerticalAlignment.Top,
            SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
        })
    end

    local function padding(l, t, r, b)
        return new("UIPadding", {
            PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, l or 0), PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, t or 0),
            PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, r or 0), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, b or 0),
        })
    end

    -- props.Wrap = ตัดบรรทัดได้ (ความสูงคงที่) · props.Auto = ตัดบรรทัด + ขยายความสูงอัตโนมัติ
    local function label(props)
        local wrapped = props.Wrap or props.Auto
        return new("TextLabel", {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Font = props.Font or FONT_BODY,
            TextSize = props.TextSize or 12,
            TextColor3 = props.Color or C.Text,
            TextXAlignment = props.Align or Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            TextWrapped = wrapped and true or false,
            TextTruncate = props.Auto and Enum.TextTruncate.None or Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
            Text = props.Text or "",
            Size = props.Size or UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
            Position = props.Position or UDim2.new(),
            AnchorPoint = props.AnchorPoint or Vector2.new(),
            LayoutOrder = props.LayoutOrder or 0,
            AutomaticSize = props.AutomaticSize or (props.Auto and Enum.AutomaticSize.Y or Enum.AutomaticSize.None),
            Parent = props.Parent,
        })
    end

    local function box(props, fill, outline, radius, thickness)
        local class = props.Class or "Frame"
        props.Class = nil
        local f = new(class, props)
        f.BackgroundColor3 = fill or C.Panel
        f.BorderSizePixel = 0
        if radius then corner(radius).Parent = f end
        if outline then
            local s = stroke(outline, thickness or 2)
            s.Parent = f
        end
        return f
    end

    -- ทำให้ลาก/แตะได้ (แตะ = onTap, ลาก = ย้ายตำแหน่ง ไม่เด้งเปิดเมนู)
    local function uiDraggable(handle, target, onTap)
        local dragging, moved, dragStart, startPos = false, false, nil, nil
        handle.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
                or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                dragging, moved = true, false
                dragStart, startPos = input.Position, target.Position
                input.Changed:Connect(function()
                    if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                        dragging = false
                        if not moved and onTap then onTap() end
                    end
                end)
            end
        end)
        bind(UserInputService.InputChanged, function(input)
            if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
                or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement) then
                local d = input.Position - dragStart
                if d.Magnitude > 6 then moved = true end
                if moved then
                    target.Position = UDim2.new(
                        startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + d.X,
                        startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + d.Y)
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    -- ระบบสลับภาษา ไทย / อังกฤษ (ปุ่มขวาบน)
    -- ใช้ tr(textLabel, "ไทย", "English") เพื่อให้ข้อความนั้นเปลี่ยนตามภาษา
    -- ฟังก์ชันข้างนอกใช้ได้ผ่าน getgenv().NAPHUB_UI  (Tr / SetLang / GetLang / OnLang)
    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    local LANG = "TH"
    local I18N = {}
    local LANG_CALLBACKS = {}

    local function pick(entry)
        local t = (LANG == "TH") and entry.th or (entry.en or entry.th)
        if entry.upper then t = t:upper() end
        return t
    end

    local function tr(inst, th, en, upper)
        local entry = { inst = inst, th = th, en = en, upper = upper }
        I18N[#I18N + 1] = entry
        inst.Text = pick(entry)
        return inst
    end

    local function setLang(l)
        if l ~= "TH" and l ~= "EN" then return end
        LANG = l
        for _, e in ipairs(I18N) do
            if e.inst and e.inst.Parent then
                e.inst.Text = pick(e)
            end
        end
        for _, cb in ipairs(LANG_CALLBACKS) do
            pcall(cb, LANG)
        end
    end

    -- ชื่อหมวดภาษาอังกฤษ (ใช้ตอนกดสลับเป็น EN)
    local TAB_EN = {
        ["ตกปลา"] = "Fishing", ["ตั้งค่า"] = "Settings",
        ["ธีมสี"] = "Theme", ["ตัวละคร"] = "Character",
    }
    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    -- ScreenGui
    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    ScreenGui = new("ScreenGui", {
        Name = "NapHubUI",
        ResetOnSpawn = false,
        IgnoreGuiInset = true,
        DisplayOrder = 2147483646, -- ซ้อนบนสุด เหนือเมนูของเกม
        ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling,
    })
    ScreenGui.Parent = GuiParent

    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    -- กรอบหน้าต่าง + เงา
    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    local root = new("Frame", {
        Name = "Window",
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
        Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(W.Width, W.Height),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Parent = ScreenGui,
    })
    MainFrame = root

    -- ย่อขนาดหน้าต่างอัตโนมัติถ้าจอเล็ก (มือถือแนวนอน)
    local function fitWindow()
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        local vp = cam and cam.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(1280, 720)
        root.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(
            math.clamp(vp.X - 24, 380, W.Width),
            math.clamp(vp.Y - 24, 260, W.Height)
        )
    end
    fitWindow()
    pcall(function()
        bind(workspace.CurrentCamera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"), fitWindow)
    end)

    box({
        Name = "Shadow",
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(W.Shadow, W.Shadow),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -W.Shadow, 1, -W.Shadow),
        BackgroundTransparency = 0.45,
        Parent = root,
    }, C.Shadow, nil, W.Radius)

    local body = box({
        Name = "Body",
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -W.Shadow, 1, -W.Shadow),
        ClipsDescendants = true,
        Parent = root,
    }, C.Body, hex("6A6A6A"), W.Radius, W.Stroke)

    local gui = ScreenGui

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- เอฟเฟกต์สายฟ้าดำรอบกรอบเมนู (เรืองแสง + ไฟวิ่งรอบขอบ + สายฟ้าแลบสุ่ม)
-- ปิดทั้งหมดได้โดยตั้ง LIGHTNING = false
do
    local LIGHTNING = true
    local BOLT_CORE = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)         -- แกนสายฟ้า (ดำ)
    local BOLT_GLOW = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150)   -- ขอบแสงจาง ๆ รอบสายฟ้า (เทา) ให้เห็นสายฟ้าดำบนพื้นดำ
    local BW, BH = W.Width - W.Shadow, W.Height - W.Shadow

    -- 1) เส้นขอบเป็นสีดำ มีแสงสว่างวิ่งวนรอบกรอบ
    local bodyStroke = body:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
    if bodyStroke and LIGHTNING then
        bodyStroke.Color = C.White
        bodyStroke.Thickness = 2
        local g = new("UIGradient", {
            Color = ColorSequence.new({
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(110, 110, 110)),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)),
            }),
            Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.55),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.55),
            }),
            Parent = bodyStroke,
        })
        local conn
        conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not gui.Parent then
                conn:Disconnect()
                return
            end
            g.Rotation = (os.clock() * 140) % 360
        end)
    end

    if LIGHTNING then

    -- 2) เงาดำด้านนอกกรอบ (กะพริบเบา ๆ)
    local glow = new("Frame", {
        Name = "Glow", BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -W.Shadow, 1, -W.Shadow),
        ZIndex = 0, Parent = root,
    }, { corner(W.Radius) })
    local gs = new("UIStroke", {
        Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0), Thickness = 6, Transparency = 0.35,
        ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border, Parent = glow,
    })
    TweenService:Create(gs, TweenInfo.new(1.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1, true), {
        Transparency = 0.88,
    }):Play()

    -- 3) สายฟ้าแลบตามขอบกรอบ
    local layer = new("Frame", {
        Name = "Lightning", BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -W.Shadow, 1, -W.Shadow),
        ZIndex = 10, Parent = root,
    })

    local function seg(parent, a, b, thick, color, transp)
        local d = b - a
        local f = Instance.new("Frame")
        f.BorderSizePixel = 0
        f.BackgroundColor3 = color
        f.BackgroundTransparency = transp
        f.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        f.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(d.Magnitude + thick * 0.5, thick)
        local m = (a + b) / 2
        f.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(m.X, m.Y)
        f.Rotation = math.deg(math.atan2(d.Y, d.X))
        f.Parent = parent
        return f
    end

    -- ขอบทั้ง 4: จุดเริ่ม + ทิศทาง (เดินตามเข็มนาฬิกา)
    local edges = {
        { Vector2.new(0, 0),   Vector2.new(1, 0),  BW },
        { Vector2.new(BW, 0),  Vector2.new(0, 1),  BH },
        { Vector2.new(BW, BH), Vector2.new(-1, 0), BW },
        { Vector2.new(0, BH),  Vector2.new(0, -1), BH },
    }

    local function drawPath(parent, pts, thickGlow, thickCore)
        for i = 1, #pts - 1 do
            seg(parent, pts[i], pts[i + 1], thickGlow, BOLT_GLOW, 0.6)
            seg(parent, pts[i], pts[i + 1], thickCore, BOLT_CORE, 0)
        end
    end

    local function spawnBolt()
        local r = math.random() * (2 * (BW + BH))
        local idx = 4
        for i = 1, 4 do
            if r <= edges[i][3] then idx = i break end
            r = r - edges[i][3]
        end
        local start, dir, elen = edges[idx][1], edges[idx][2], edges[idx][3]
        local normal = Vector2.new(dir.Y, -dir.X) -- ชี้ออกนอกกรอบ
        local L = math.random(70, 160)
        local t0 = math.random() * math.max(elen - L, 0)
        local n = math.max(math.floor(L / 8), 4)

        local holder = new("Frame", {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 10, Parent = layer,
        })

        local pts = {}
        for i = 0, n do
            local off = (i == 0 or i == n) and 0 or (math.random() * 2 - 1) * 7
            pts[i + 1] = start + dir * (t0 + L * i / n) + normal * off
        end
        drawPath(holder, pts, 5, 1.5)

        -- กิ่งแยกสั้น ๆ
        if math.random() < 0.65 then
            local k = math.random(2, n)
            local side = (math.random() < 0.5) and -1 or 1
            local bdir = (normal * side + dir * (math.random() * 2 - 1) * 0.7).Unit
            local bp = { pts[k] }
            for i = 1, 3 do
                bp[i + 1] = bp[i] + bdir * 8 + Vector2.new(math.random() * 6 - 3, math.random() * 6 - 3)
            end
            drawPath(holder, bp, 4, 1)
        end

        -- กะพริบแล้วหายไป
        task.delay(0.06, function() if holder.Parent then holder.Visible = false end end)
        task.delay(0.10, function() if holder.Parent then holder.Visible = true end end)
        task.delay(0.20, function() holder:Destroy() end)
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        while gui.Parent do
            task.wait(0.08 + math.random() * 0.22)
            if gui.Parent and root.Visible then
                pcall(spawnBolt)
                if math.random() < 0.4 then pcall(spawnBolt) end
            end
        end
    end)
    end -- if LIGHTNING
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- เลเยอร์พื้นหลัง: รูป (ฝั่งฟังก์ชัน) + ดาว  (อยู่หลังทุกอย่าง)
----------------------------------------------------------------
local bgAsset = loadBackground()
if bgAsset then
    local bgImg = new("ImageLabel", {
        Name = "Background",
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Image = bgAsset,
        ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Stretch,
        ImageTransparency = 0,
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1),
        Position = UDim2.new(1, -4, 1, -W.Ground + IMAGE_OFFSET_Y),
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(IMAGE_W, IMAGE_H),
        ZIndex = 1,
        Parent = body,
    })
    -- เฟดขอบบน/ซ้ายของรูปให้กลืนกับพื้นดำ
    new("UIGradient", {
        Rotation = 45,
        Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.4, 0.2),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0),
        }),
        Parent = bgImg,
    })
end

local stars = new("Frame", {
    Name = "Stars",
    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
    Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
    ClipsDescendants = true,
    ZIndex = 1,
    Parent = body,
})

-- ดาวระยิบ (จุดเล็ก ๆ กะพริบ)
for _ = 1, 18 do
    local sz = math.random(1, 2)
    local d = box({
        BackgroundTransparency = 0.7,
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(sz, sz),
        Position = UDim2.fromScale(math.random(), math.random()),
        ZIndex = 1, Parent = stars,
    }, C.White, nil, UDim.new(1, 0))
    task.delay(math.random() * 2, function()
        if d.Parent then
            TweenService:Create(d, TweenInfo.new(1 + math.random() * 1.8, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1, true), {
                BackgroundTransparency = 0.05,
            }):Play()
        end
    end)
end

-- ดาวตก
local ANGLE = 30
local function shootingStar()
    local len = math.random(50, 95)
    local startX = math.random(math.floor(W.Width * 0.35), W.Width + 40)
    local startY = math.random(-30, math.floor(W.Height * 0.4))
    local dist = math.random(240, 380)
    local dx = dist * math.cos(math.rad(ANGLE))
    local dy = dist * math.sin(math.rad(ANGLE))
    local t = 0.8 + math.random() * 0.7

    local star = new("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = C.White,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(len, 2),
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(startX, startY),
        Rotation = -ANGLE,
        ZIndex = 1,
        Parent = stars,
    })
    corner(UDim.new(1, 0)).Parent = star
    new("UIGradient", { -- หัว (ซ้าย) สว่าง หาง (ขวา) จางหาย
        Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
        }),
        Parent = star,
    })
    TweenService:Create(star, TweenInfo.new(t, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), {
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(startX - dx, startY + dy),
    }):Play()
    TweenService:Create(star, TweenInfo.new(t * 0.45, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.In, 0, false, t * 0.55), {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
    }):Play()
    task.delay(t + 0.1, function() star:Destroy() end)
end

task.spawn(function()
    while gui.Parent do
        task.wait(0.7 + math.random() * 1.3)
        if gui.Parent and root.Visible then pcall(shootingStar) end
    end
end)

    local langLbl
    do
----------------------------------------------------------------

    ClipsDescendants = true,
    Active = true,
    ZIndex = 5,
    Parent = body,
}, C.Topbar, nil, W.Radius)

box({ -- ปิดมุมล่างของแถบบนให้เป็นเหลี่ยม
    Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -W.Radius),
    Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, W.Radius),
    ZIndex = 5, Parent = topbar,
}, C.Topbar)

box({ -- เส้นขอบล่างแถบบน
    AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1),
    Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1),
    Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 2),
    ZIndex = 6, Parent = topbar,
}, C.Outline)

local left = new("Frame", {
    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
    Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 0),
    Size = UDim2.new(1, -124, 1, -2),
    ZIndex = 6, Parent = topbar,
}, { list(8, true, Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Left, Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center) })

-- โลโก้มุมซ้ายบน (รูป NAP) — ถ้าโหลดรูปไม่ได้จะแสดงตัว N แทน
local logoAsset = loadLogo()
local emblem = box({
    Class = "ImageLabel",
    Name = "Emblem",
    Size = UDim2.fromOffset(30, 30),
    Image = logoAsset or "",
    ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit,
    BackgroundTransparency = logoAsset and 1 or 0,
    LayoutOrder = 0, ZIndex = 6, Parent = left,
}, C.PanelHeader, nil, 6)
if not logoAsset then
    label({
        Text = "N", Font = FONT, TextSize = 14, Color = C.White,
        Align = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, Parent = emblem,
    }).ZIndex = 7
end

-- ชื่อหัวเมนู (ตัวอักษรขาว)
local titleHolder = new("Frame", {
    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
    AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X,
    Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 30),
    LayoutOrder = 1, ZIndex = 6, Parent = left,
}, { list(0, true, Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Left, Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center) })

local wordAsset = loadWord()
if wordAsset then
    -- ใช้ตัวอักษร NAP HUB จากรูปเดียวกับลูกลอย
    new("ImageLabel", {
        Name = "WordMark",
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Image = wordAsset,
        ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit,
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(74, 28),
        ZIndex = 6, Parent = titleHolder,
    })
else
for i, ch in utf8.codes("NAPHUB") do
    local c = utf8.char(ch)
    local isSpace = c == " "
    local slot = new("Frame", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(isSpace and 6 or 13, 24),
        LayoutOrder = i, ZIndex = 6, Parent = titleHolder,
    })
    local t = label({
        Text = c, Font = FONT, TextSize = 16,
        Color = C.White,
        Align = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, Parent = slot,
    })
    t.ZIndex = 6
    new("UIStroke", { -- เรืองแสงจาง ๆ รอบตัวอักษร
        Color = hex("8A8A8A"), Thickness = 1, Transparency = 0.55,
        ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Contextual, Parent = t,
    })
end
end

    Name = "Pill",
    AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X,
    Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 16),
    LayoutOrder = 2, ZIndex = 6, Parent = left,
}, C.Element, C.Outline, UDim.new(1, 0), 1)
padding(7, 0, 7, 0).Parent = pill

    Align = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
    AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1),
    Parent = pill,
}).ZIndex = 7

-- ปุ่มขวาบน (หน้าตาเท่านั้น)
local right = new("Frame", {
    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
    AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0),
    Position = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 0),
    Size = UDim2.new(0, 104, 1, -2),
    ZIndex = 6, Parent = topbar,
}, { list(6, true, Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right, Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center) })


local function topButton(order, text, face, ink, width, onClick)
    local b = box({
        Name = text,
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(width or 26, 26),
        LayoutOrder = order, ZIndex = 6, Parent = right,
    }, face, C.Line, 7, 1)
    local lbl = label({
        Text = text, Font = FONT, TextSize = 12, Color = ink,
        Align = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, Parent = b,
    })
    lbl.ZIndex = 7
    local hit = new("TextButton", {
        Name = "Hit", Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false,
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
        ZIndex = 9, Parent = b,
    })
    hit.Activated:Connect(function()
        if onClick then onClick() end
    end)
    return b, lbl
end

local langBtn
langBtn, langLbl = topButton(0, "TH", C.Element, C.Text, 34, function() end)
langBtn.Name = "LangButton"
langBtn.Visible = false   -- เมนูเป็นภาษาไทยอย่างเดียว ไม่มีปุ่มสลับภาษา
topButton(1, "–", C.Element, C.Text, 26, function() Window:Close() end)
topButton(2, "✕", C.White, C.Black, 26, function() Window:Close() end)

-- ลากหน้าต่าง (เมาส์ + ทัช)
uiDraggable(topbar, root, nil)

    end

    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    -- แถบข้าง (Sidebar)
    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    local sidebar = box({
        Name = "Sidebar",
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, W.Topbar),
        Size = UDim2.new(0, W.Sidebar, 1, -(W.Topbar + W.Ground)),
        BackgroundTransparency = 0.15,
        ZIndex = 2, Parent = body,
    }, C.Sidebar)

    box({ -- เส้นขอบขวา
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.fromScale(1, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 2, 1, 0), ZIndex = 4, Parent = sidebar,
    }, C.Outline)
    box({ -- แถบเงาด้านใน
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, -2, 0, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 4, 1, 0), ZIndex = 3, Parent = sidebar,
    }, C.SidebarAlt)

    -- รายการแท็บเลื่อนขึ้นลงได้ (แท็บเยอะ)
    local tabHolder = new("ScrollingFrame", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 6),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 1, -(W.UserCard + 6)),
        ScrollBarThickness = 2,
        ScrollBarImageColor3 = C.White,
        ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y,
        CanvasSize = UDim2.new(),
        AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
        ZIndex = 3, Parent = sidebar,
    }, {
        list(3),
        padding(6, 2, 6, 2),
    })

    do
        local l = label({
            Font = FONT, TextSize = 10, Color = C.SidebarMuted,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16), LayoutOrder = -1, Parent = tabHolder,
        })
        l.ZIndex = 3
        tr(l, "หมวดฟังก์ชัน", "Functions", true)
    end

    -- ชุดไอคอนของเมนู (สัญลักษณ์ขาว-ดำ) ผูกกับชื่อ Icon ที่ฟังก์ชันส่งมา
    local ICON = {
        Home = "⌂", Combat = "⚔", Farm = "✿", Visuals = "◉",
        Teleport = "➤", Settings = "⚙", Misc = "◈", Star = "★",
    }
    local ICON_BY_NAME = {
        home = ICON.Home, sparkles = ICON.Farm, settings = ICON.Settings,
        palette = ICON.Misc, eye = ICON.Visuals, move = ICON.Teleport, user = ICON.Star,
    }

    -- ป้ายไอคอนสี่เหลี่ยมมน: active = พื้นสีเน้น ไอคอนดำ · ปกติ = พื้นเข้ม ไอคอนขาว (สีเน้นเปลี่ยนตามธีมสี)
    local function makeBadge(parent, x, y, size, glyph, z)
        z = z or 4
        local badge = box({
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size),
            ZIndex = z, Parent = parent,
        }, C.SidebarAlt, nil, 6)
        local bs = stroke(C.Outline, 1)
        bs.Parent = badge
        local gl = label({
            Text = glyph, Font = FONT, TextSize = size - 9, Color = C.White,
            Align = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, Parent = badge,
        })
        gl.ZIndex = z + 1
        local obj = {}
        function obj.set(active)
            badge.BackgroundColor3 = active and Accent or C.SidebarAlt
            bs.Enabled = not active
            gl.TextColor3 = active and C.Black or C.White
        end
        obj.set(false)
        return obj
    end

    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    -- การ์ดผู้ใช้
    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    do
        local userCard = new("Frame", {
            Name = "User",
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1),
            Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1),
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, W.UserCard),
            ZIndex = 3, Parent = sidebar,
        })
        box({ Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1), ZIndex = 3, Parent = userCard }, C.Outline)
        -- รูปตัวละครของผู้เล่น (ดึงจาก Roblox) — เปลี่ยนเป็น Enum.ThumbnailType.HeadShot ได้ถ้าอยากได้เฉพาะหน้า
        local AVATAR_TYPE = Enum.ThumbnailType.AvatarBust
        local avatarFrame = box({
            Name = "Avatar",
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
            Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0.5, 0),
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(34, 34),
            ClipsDescendants = true,
            ZIndex = 3, Parent = userCard,
        }, C.PanelHeader, C.Line, UDim.new(1, 0), 1)
        local avatarImg = new("ImageLabel", {
            Name = "AvatarImage",
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
            ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop,
            Image = "",
            ZIndex = 4, Parent = avatarFrame,
        })
        local avatarFallback = label({
            Text = "?", Font = FONT, TextSize = 14, Color = C.White,
            Align = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, Parent = avatarFrame,
        })
        avatarFallback.ZIndex = 4
        task.spawn(function()
            if not LocalPlayer then return end
            local ok, img = pcall(function()
                return Players:GetUserThumbnailAsync(LocalPlayer.UserId, AVATAR_TYPE, Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size150x150)
            end)
            if ok and img and img ~= "" then
                avatarImg.Image = img
                avatarFallback.Visible = false
            end
        end)
        label({
            Text = LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.DisplayName or "Player",
            Font = FONT, TextSize = 12, Color = C.White,
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(50, 7), Size = UDim2.new(1, -54, 0, 15),
            Parent = userCard,
        }).ZIndex = 4
        label({
            Text = "@" .. (LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Name or "player"),
            TextSize = 10, Color = C.SidebarMuted,
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(50, 24), Size = UDim2.new(1, -54, 0, 13),
            Parent = userCard,
        }).ZIndex = 4
    end

    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    -- พื้นที่เนื้อหา (Main) + หัวข้อหน้า + ช่องค้นหา
    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    local main = new("Frame", {
        Name = "Main",
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(W.Sidebar, W.Topbar),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -W.Sidebar, 1, -(W.Topbar + W.Ground)),
        ZIndex = 2, Parent = body,
    })

    local pageTitle = label({
        Font = FONT, TextSize = 17, Color = C.White,
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 6), Size = UDim2.new(1, -170, 0, 22),
        Parent = main,
    })
    local pageDesc = label({
        TextSize = 11, Color = C.SubText,
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 27), Size = UDim2.new(1, -170, 0, 14),
        Parent = main,
    })

    local search = box({
        Name = "Search",
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, W.Header / 2),
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(140, 26),
        ZIndex = 3, Parent = main,
    }, C.Element, C.Line, UDim.new(1, 0), 1)
    label({
        Text = "⌕", Font = FONT, TextSize = 14, Color = C.White,
        Align = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 26),
        Parent = search,
    }).ZIndex = 4
    local searchBox = new("TextBox", {
        Name = "SearchBox",
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Font = FONT_BODY, TextSize = 12,
        TextColor3 = C.Text, PlaceholderColor3 = C.Muted,
        Text = "", PlaceholderText = "ค้นหา...",
        ClearTextOnFocus = false,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(26, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -32, 1, 0),
        ZIndex = 4, Parent = search,
    })
    LANG_CALLBACKS[#LANG_CALLBACKS + 1] = function(l)
        searchBox.PlaceholderText = (l == "TH") and "ค้นหา..." or "Search..."
    end

    local ActiveTab = nil

    local function applyHeader()
        if ActiveTab then
            pageTitle.Text = (LANG == "TH") and ActiveTab.TitleTH or ActiveTab.TitleEN
        end
        pageDesc.Text = (LANG == "TH") and "จัดการฟังก์ชันในหมวดนี้" or "Manage features in this category"
    end
    LANG_CALLBACKS[#LANG_CALLBACKS + 1] = applyHeader

    -- ค้นหา: กรองแถวในทุกหมวดตามข้อความ (ว่าง = แสดงทั้งหมด)
    local function applySearch()
        local q = string.lower(searchBox.Text or "")
        q = (q:gsub("^%s+", ""))
        q = (q:gsub("%s+$", ""))
        for _, t in ipairs(Tabs) do
            for _, r in ipairs(t.Rows) do
                if q == "" or r.section then
                    r.frame.Visible = true
                else
                    r.frame.Visible = string.find(r.text, q, 1, true) ~= nil
                end
            end
            -- ซ่อนการ์ดที่ไม่มีแถวตรงกับคำค้น
            for _, c in ipairs(t.Cards or {}) do
                local any = (q == "")
                if not any then
                    for _, r in ipairs(t.Rows) do
                        if r.card == c and not r.section and r.frame.Visible then any = true break end
                    end
                end
                c.Frame.Visible = any
            end
        end
    end
    searchBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(applySearch)


    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    -- สไตล์กล่องฟังก์ชัน (Groupbox) แบบเมนูใหม่: ดำ + แสงวิ่งวนรอบกรอบ
    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    local GB_HEAD = Color3.fromRGB(12, 12, 12)
    local GB_GRADS = {}
    do
        local conn
        conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not ScreenGui or not ScreenGui.Parent then conn:Disconnect() return end
            local rot = (os.clock() * 140) % 360
            for _, g in ipairs(GB_GRADS) do
                if g.Parent then g.Rotation = rot end
            end
        end)
    end
    local function styleCardStroke(card)
        local st = card:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
        if not st then return end
        st.Color = C.White
        st.Thickness = 1.5
        GB_GRADS[#GB_GRADS + 1] = new("UIGradient", {
            Color = ColorSequence.new({
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(110, 110, 110)),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)),
            }),
            Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.5),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.5),
            }),
            Parent = st,
        })
    end
    
    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    -- แท็บ + ตัวควบคุม (ใช้ API เดิมของต้นฉบับ)
    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    local function makeTab(title, icon)
        local idx = #Tabs + 1
        local tab = {
            Title = title,
            Icon = icon,
            TitleTH = tostring(title),
            TitleEN = TAB_EN[title] or tostring(title),
            Controls = {},
            Rows = {},
        }

        -- หน้าเนื้อหาของแท็บ: เลื่อนขึ้นลงได้ (tab.Frame = ที่วางตัวควบคุม)
        tab.Frame = new("ScrollingFrame", {
            Name = "Tab_" .. tostring(idx),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, W.Header),
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -W.Header),
            ScrollBarThickness = 3,
            ScrollBarImageColor3 = C.White,
            ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y,
            CanvasSize = UDim2.new(),
            AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
            Visible = false,
            ZIndex = 2, Parent = main,
        })

        -- ===== เลย์เอาต์ 2 คอลัมน์ (การ์ดแต่ละหัวข้อ) =====
        -- คอลัมน์ซ้าย/ขวา "ตรึงขอบ" (ซ้ายชิดซ้าย · ขวาชิดขวา) ไม่พึ่ง UIListLayout แนวนอน
        -- เพื่อให้พอดีกรอบเสมอ ไม่ล้นขอบขวา
        local COL_GAP = 8
        -- ระยะขอบซ้าย 10 / ขวา 12 ลบออกจากความกว้างเองเลย (ไม่ใช้ UIPadding ใน ScrollingFrame
        -- เพราะสเกลความกว้างไม่หักขอบ ทำให้คอลัมน์ขวาล้น)
        local cols = new("Frame", {
            Name = "Columns", BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 4),
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -22, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
            ClipsDescendants = false,
            ZIndex = 2, Parent = tab.Frame,
        }, { padding(0, 0, 0, 10) })
        local stackList = new("UIListLayout", {   -- ใช้เฉพาะโหมดหน้าต่างแคบ (เรียงลงมา)
            FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Vertical,
            Padding = UDim.new(0, 8),
            SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
        })
        local function makeCol(order)
            return new("Frame", {
                Name = "Col" .. order, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
                LayoutOrder = order, ZIndex = 2, Parent = cols,
            }, { list(8) })
        end
        local colA, colB = makeCol(1), makeCol(2)
        local colH = { 0, 0 }   -- ความสูงโดยประมาณของแต่ละคอลัมน์ (ไว้จัดการ์ดให้สมดุล)
        tab.Cards = {}
        local curCard = nil

        local function relayout()
            local aw = cols.AbsoluteSize.X
            local narrow = aw > 0 and aw < 300
            if narrow then
                stackList.Parent = cols
                colA.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
                colB.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
                colA.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
                colB.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
                colA.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
                colB.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
            else
                stackList.Parent = nil
                colA.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
                colA.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
                colA.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -COL_GAP / 2, 0, 0)
                colB.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
                colB.Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
                colB.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -COL_GAP / 2, 0, 0)
            end
        end
        relayout()
        cols:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(relayout)

        -- สร้างการ์ดใหม่ วางในคอลัมน์ที่สั้นกว่า
        local function newCard(titleText, forceCol)
            local idx = forceCol or ((colH[1] <= colH[2]) and 1 or 2)
            local frame = box({
                Name = "Card", Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
                BackgroundTransparency = 0.3, ZIndex = 3, Parent = (idx == 1) and colA or colB,
            }, C.Panel, C.White, 9, 1)
            frame.ClipsDescendants = true
            styleCardStroke(frame)
            list(4).Parent = frame
            padding(5, 5, 5, 5).Parent = frame
            colH[idx] = colH[idx] + 10
            curCard = { Frame = frame, idx = idx }
            table.insert(tab.Cards, curCard)
            return curCard
        end

        -- ที่วางตัวควบคุมตัวถัดไป (= การ์ดปัจจุบัน) · h = ความสูงโดยประมาณ
        local function host(h)
            if not curCard then newCard(nil) end
            colH[curCard.idx] = colH[curCard.idx] + (h or 40) + 4
            return curCard.Frame
        end
        function tab:Host(h) return host(h) end

        -- ปุ่มแท็บในแถบข้าง
        local btn = box({
            Class = "TextButton",
            Name = "Tab_" .. tostring(title),
            Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            LayoutOrder = idx, ZIndex = 3, Parent = tabHolder,
        }, C.TabActive, nil, 7)
        local btnStroke = stroke(C.Line, 1)
        btnStroke.Enabled = false
        btnStroke.Parent = btn
        local badge = makeBadge(btn, 4, 4, 22, ICON_BY_NAME[icon] or ICON[icon] or ICON.Misc, 4)
        local tl = label({
            Font = FONT, TextSize = 12, Color = C.White,
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(32, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -36, 1, 0),
            Parent = btn,
        })
        tl.ZIndex = 4
        tr(tl, tab.TitleTH, tab.TitleEN)

        local function renderSide(selected)
            btn.BackgroundTransparency = selected and 0 or 1
            btnStroke.Color = selected and Accent or C.Line
            btnStroke.Enabled = selected
            badge.set(selected)
        end
        renderSide(false)
        tab.RenderSide = renderSide
        table.insert(AccentHooks, function()
            renderSide(ActiveTab == tab)
        end)

        local function selectTab()
            for _, t in ipairs(Tabs) do
                t.Frame.Visible = false
                t.RenderSide(false)
            end
            tab.Frame.Visible = true
            renderSide(true)
            ActiveTab = tab
            applyHeader()
        end
        btn.Activated:Connect(selectTab)
        table.insert(Tabs, tab)

        function tab:Select() selectTab() end

        -- เก็บแถวไว้ให้ช่องค้นหากรอง
        local function reg(frame, text, isSection)
            table.insert(tab.Rows, {
                frame = frame,
                text = string.lower(tostring(text or "")),
                section = isSection == true,
                card = curCard,
            })
        end

        -- แถวพื้นฐาน (กล่องกึ่งโปร่งใสให้เห็นรูป/ดาวด้านหลัง)
        local function rowBase(height, text)
            local row = box({
                Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, height),
                BackgroundTransparency = 0.45,
                ZIndex = 3, Parent = host(height),
            }, C.Element, nil, 6)
            reg(row, text, false)
            return row
        end

        -- หัวข้อกลุ่ม (Groupbox header)
        function tab:Section(opt)
            local title_ = tostring(opt and opt.Title or "")
            newCard(title_, opt and opt.Column)
            colH[curCard.idx] = colH[curCard.idx] + 32
            local f = box({
                Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28),
                BackgroundTransparency = 0.15,
                ZIndex = 3, Parent = curCard.Frame,
            }, GB_HEAD, C.Outline, 8, 1)
            local sb = makeBadge(f, 5, 4, 20, ICON.Star, 4)
            sb.set(true)
            table.insert(AccentHooks, function() sb.set(true) end)
            label({
                Text = title_, Font = FONT, TextSize = 11, Color = C.White,
                Position = UDim2.fromOffset(30, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -38, 1, 0),
                Parent = f,
            }).ZIndex = 4
            reg(f, title_, true)
            return f
        end

        -- ปุ่ม
        function tab:Button(opt)
            local title_ = tostring(opt and opt.Title or "")
            local wrap = new("Frame", {
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40), ZIndex = 3, Parent = host(40),
            })
            reg(wrap, title_, false)
            box({ -- เงาปุ่ม (ลึก 3px)
                Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 3), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 34), ZIndex = 3, Parent = wrap,
            }, C.Shadow, C.Outline, 7, 1)
            local face = box({
                Class = "TextButton", Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false,
                Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 34), ZIndex = 4, Parent = wrap,
            }, hex("2A2A2A"), C.Line, 7, 1)
            label({
                Text = title_, Font = FONT, TextSize = 11, Color = C.White, Wrap = true,
                Align = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
                Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, 0), Parent = face,
            }).ZIndex = 5
            face.Activated:Connect(function()
                face.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 2)
                task.delay(0.08, function()
                    if face.Parent then face.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0) end
                end)
                if opt and opt.Callback then pcall(opt.Callback) end
            end)
            return face
        end

        -- สวิตช์ (ค่า true/false ทั้งหมดใช้ตัวนี้)
        function tab:Toggle(opt)
            local state = opt and opt.Value == true
            local title_ = tostring(opt and opt.Title or "")
            local row = rowBase(40, title_)
            label({
                Text = title_, TextSize = 11, Color = C.Text, Wrap = true,
                Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -64, 1, 0), Parent = row,
            }).ZIndex = 4
            local track = box({
                AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0.5, 0),
                Size = UDim2.fromOffset(36, 18), ZIndex = 4, Parent = row,
            }, C.Track, C.Line, UDim.new(1, 0), 1)
            local knob = box({
                AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
                Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14), ZIndex = 5, Parent = track,
            }, C.White, nil, UDim.new(1, 0))
            local hit = new("TextButton", {
                Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 8, Parent = row,
            })

            local control = {}
            local function render()
                if state then
                    track.BackgroundColor3 = Accent
                    knob.BackgroundColor3 = C.Black
                    knob.Position = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0.5, 0)
                else
                    track.BackgroundColor3 = C.Track
                    knob.BackgroundColor3 = C.White
                    knob.Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, 0)
                end
            end
            table.insert(AccentHooks, render)
            local function set(v, fire)
                state = v == true
                render()
                if fire and opt and opt.Callback then
                    pcall(opt.Callback, state)
                    autoSaveConfig()
                end
            end
            function control:SetValue(v) set(v, true) end
            function control:Set(v) set(v, true) end
            function control:GetValue() return state end
            hit.Activated:Connect(function() set(not state, true) end)
            render()
            return control
        end

        -- ดรอปดาวน์
        function tab:Dropdown(opt)
            local values = opt and opt.Values or {}
            local current = opt and opt.Value
            if current == nil then current = values[1] end
            local multi = opt and opt.Multi == true
            if multi and type(current) ~= "table" then current = current and { current } or {} end
            local title_ = tostring(opt and opt.Title or "")

            local holder = new("Frame", {
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 44),
                AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
                ZIndex = 3, Parent = host(44),
            }, { list(4) })
            reg(holder, title_, false)

            local head = box({
                Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 44), BackgroundTransparency = 0.2,
                LayoutOrder = 0, ZIndex = 3, Parent = holder,
            }, C.Element, C.Line, 7, 1)
            label({
                Text = title_, Font = FONT, TextSize = 10, Color = C.SubText,
                Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 4), Size = UDim2.new(1, -34, 0, 15), Parent = head,
            }).ZIndex = 4
            local valueLabel = label({
                TextSize = 11, Color = C.Text,
                Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 21), Size = UDim2.new(1, -34, 0, 18), Parent = head,
            })
            valueLabel.ZIndex = 4
            local arrow = label({
                Text = "▾", Font = FONT, TextSize = 12, Color = C.White,
                Align = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
                AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0.5, 0),
                Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 18), Parent = head,
            })
            arrow.ZIndex = 4
            local headHit = new("TextButton", {
                Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 8, Parent = head,
            })

            local listFrame = box({
                Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
                BackgroundTransparency = 0.1, Visible = false,
                LayoutOrder = 1, ZIndex = 3, Parent = holder,
            }, C.PanelHeader, C.Line, 7, 1)
            list(3).Parent = listFrame
            padding(4, 4, 4, 4).Parent = listFrame

            local function currentText()
                if multi then
                    local a = {}
                    for _, v in ipairs(current) do table.insert(a, tostring(v)) end
                    return #a > 0 and table.concat(a, ", ") or "ไม่ได้เลือก"
                end
                return tostring(current or "")
            end
            valueLabel.Text = currentText()

            local optionButtons = {}
            local function refreshOptions()
                for v, ob in pairs(optionButtons) do
                    local on = false
                    if multi then
                        for _, x in ipairs(current) do if x == v then on = true break end end
                    else
                        on = (current == v)
                    end
                    ob.BackgroundColor3 = on and C.Hover or C.Element
                end
            end

            local function fire()
                if opt and opt.Callback then pcall(opt.Callback, current); autoSaveConfig() end
            end
            local control = {}
            function control:Select(v)
                current = v
                if multi and type(v) ~= "table" then current = { v } end
                valueLabel.Text = currentText()
                refreshOptions()
                fire()
            end
            function control:Set(v) self:Select(v) end
            function control:SetValue(v) self:Select(v) end
            function control:GetValue() return current end

            for i, v in ipairs(values) do
                local ob = box({
                    Class = "TextButton", Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false,
                    Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30), LayoutOrder = i, ZIndex = 4, Parent = listFrame,
                }, C.Element, C.Outline, 6, 1)
                label({
                    Text = tostring(v), TextSize = 10, Color = C.Text, Wrap = true,
                    Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, 0), Parent = ob,
                }).ZIndex = 5
                optionButtons[v] = ob
                ob.Activated:Connect(function()
                    if multi then
                        local found = false
                        for j, x in ipairs(current) do
                            if x == v then table.remove(current, j); found = true; break end
                        end
                        if not found then table.insert(current, v) end
                    else
                        current = v
                        listFrame.Visible = false
                        arrow.Text = "▾"
                    end
                    valueLabel.Text = currentText()
                    refreshOptions()
                    fire()
                end)
            end
            refreshOptions()

            headHit.Activated:Connect(function()
                listFrame.Visible = not listFrame.Visible
                arrow.Text = listFrame.Visible and "▴" or "▾"
            end)
            return control
        end

        -- สไลเดอร์
        function tab:Slider(opt)
            local min, max, default = 0, 100, 0
            if opt and type(opt.Value) == "table" then
                min = tonumber(opt.Value.Min) or min
                max = tonumber(opt.Value.Max) or max
                default = tonumber(opt.Value.Default) or min
            else
                default = tonumber(opt and opt.Value) or min
            end
            local step = tonumber(opt and opt.Step) or 1
            if step <= 0 then step = 1 end
            local value = math.clamp(default, min, max)
            local title_ = tostring(opt and opt.Title or "")

            local row = rowBase(52, title_)
            label({
                Text = title_, TextSize = 11, Color = C.Text,
                Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 5), Size = UDim2.new(1, -70, 0, 18), Parent = row,
            }).ZIndex = 4
            local valLabel = label({
                Text = tostring(value), Font = FONT, TextSize = 11, Color = C.SubText,
                Align = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
                Position = UDim2.new(1, -62, 0, 5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(52, 18), Parent = row,
            })
            valLabel.ZIndex = 4

            local track = box({
                Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 32), Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 8), ZIndex = 4, Parent = row,
            }, C.Track, C.Outline, UDim.new(1, 0), 1)
            local fill = box({
                Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1), ZIndex = 5, Parent = track,
            }, Accent, nil, UDim.new(1, 0))
            table.insert(AccentHooks, function() fill.BackgroundColor3 = Accent end)
            local knob = box({
                AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0.5),
                Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14), ZIndex = 6, Parent = track,
            }, C.White, C.Black, UDim.new(1, 0), 2)

            local function render()
                local a = (max > min) and ((value - min) / (max - min)) or 0
                fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(a, 1)
                knob.Position = UDim2.fromScale(a, 0.5)
                valLabel.Text = tostring(value)
            end
            local function set(v, fire)
                local raw = math.clamp(tonumber(v) or value, min, max)
                local snapped = min + math.floor(((raw - min) / step) + 0.5) * step
                snapped = math.floor(math.clamp(snapped, min, max) * 1000 + 0.5) / 1000
                local changed = (snapped ~= value)
                value = snapped
                render()
                if fire and changed and opt and opt.Callback then
                    pcall(opt.Callback, value)
                    autoSaveConfig()
                end
            end

            local dragging = false
            local function fromX(x)
                local w = math.max(track.AbsoluteSize.X, 1)
                local a = math.clamp((x - track.AbsolutePosition.X) / w, 0, 1)
                set(min + a * (max - min), true)
            end
            local function setScroll(on) pcall(function() tab.Frame.ScrollingEnabled = on end) end
            local hit = new("TextButton", {
                Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 8, Parent = row,
            })
            hit.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
                if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    dragging = true; setScroll(false); fromX(i.Position.X)
                end
            end)
            bind(UserInputService.InputChanged, function(i)
                if dragging and (i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                    fromX(i.Position.X)
                end
            end)
            bind(UserInputService.InputEnded, function(i)
                if dragging and (i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                    dragging = false; setScroll(true)
                end
            end)

            render()
            local control = {}
            function control:SetValue(v) set(v, true) end
            function control:Set(v) set(v, true) end
            function control:GetValue() return value end
            return control
        end

        -- กล่องข้อความสถานะ
        function tab:Paragraph(opt)
            local title_ = tostring(opt and opt.Title or "")
            local f = box({
                Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
                BackgroundTransparency = 0.2, ZIndex = 3, Parent = host(50),
            }, C.Panel, C.Outline, 8, 1)
            list(2).Parent = f
            padding(10, 6, 10, 6).Parent = f
            label({
                Text = title_, Font = FONT, TextSize = 11, Color = C.White, Auto = true,
                Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 14), LayoutOrder = 1, Parent = f,
            }).ZIndex = 4
            local bodyLbl = label({
                Text = tostring(opt and opt.Content or ""), TextSize = 10, Color = C.SubText, Auto = true,
                Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 14), LayoutOrder = 2, Parent = f,
            })
            bodyLbl.ZIndex = 4
            reg(f, title_, false)
            local control = {}
            function control:SetContent(v) bodyLbl.Text = tostring(v or "") end
            return control
        end

        return tab
    end

    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    -- Window compatibility layer: keeps the original Tab(...) calls working.
    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    function Window:Tab(opt)
        return makeTab(opt and opt.Title or "เมนู", opt and opt.Icon or "")
    end
    function Window:Open()
        MainFrame.Visible = true
    end
    function Window:Close()
        MainFrame.Visible = false
    end
    function Window:Destroy()
        pcall(function() ScreenGui:Destroy() end)
        pcall(function() ToggleGui:Destroy() end)
    end
    function Window:SelectTab(tab)
        if tab and tab.Select then tab:Select() end
    end

    local lastToggleTime = 0
    toggleMainWindow = function()
        local now = tick()
        if now - lastToggleTime < 0.22 then return end
        lastToggleTime = now
        if MainFrame.Visible then
            Window:Close()
        else
            Window:Open()
            task.defer(function()
                if Tabs[1] then Tabs[1]:Select() end
            end)
        end
    end

    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    -- แถบล่าง (Ground) + จุดจับมุม
    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    do
        local ground = box({
            Name = "Ground",
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1),
            Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1),
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, W.Ground),
            ClipsDescendants = true,
            ZIndex = 3, Parent = body,
        }, C.Topbar, nil, W.Radius)

        box({ -- ปิดมุมบนให้เป็นเหลี่ยม
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, W.Radius), ZIndex = 3, Parent = ground,
        }, C.Topbar)
        box({ -- เส้นขอบบนของแถบล่าง
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 2), ZIndex = 4, Parent = ground,
        }, C.Outline)

        label({
            Text = "◢", Font = FONT, TextSize = 11, Color = C.White,
            Align = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1), Position = UDim2.new(1, -4, 1, -1),
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 14), Parent = ground,
        }).ZIndex = 5
    end

    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    -- ปุ่มลอย (ลูกลอย): แตะเพื่อเปิด/ปิดเมนู · กดค้างแล้วลากเพื่อย้ายตำแหน่ง
    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    ToggleGui = new("ScreenGui", {
        Name = "NapHub_LootToForge_Floating",
        ResetOnSpawn = false,
        DisplayOrder = 2147483647,
        ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling,
        IgnoreGuiInset = true,
    })
    ToggleGui.Parent = GuiParent

    do
----------------------------------------------------------------
-- ปุ่มลอย (ลูกลอย): แตะเพื่อเปิด/ปิดเมนู · กดค้างแล้วลากเพื่อย้ายตำแหน่ง
----------------------------------------------------------------
local logoAsset = loadLogo()
local FLOAT = 64 -- ขนาดลูกลอย (พิกเซล) ปรับได้
local floatBtn = new("ImageButton", {
    Name = "FloatButton",
    AutoButtonColor = false,
    Active = true,
    BackgroundColor3 = C.Black,
    BackgroundTransparency = logoAsset and 1 or 0,
    BorderSizePixel = 0,
    Image = logoAsset or "",
    ImageTransparency = 0,
    ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Stretch, -- แสดงรูปเต็มพื้นที่ ไม่ย่อ/ไม่ตัด
    Size = UDim2.fromOffset(FLOAT, FLOAT),
    Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 90),
    ZIndex = 50,
    Parent = ToggleGui,
})
-- มุมโค้งตามกรอบในรูป (ซ่อนมุมดำของไฟล์ JPEG) · ไม่มีขอบเพิ่ม ใช้กรอบเรืองแสงของรูปเอง
corner(UDim.new(0.15, 0)).Parent = floatBtn
if not logoAsset then
    stroke(C.Line, 2).Parent = floatBtn
    label({
        Text = "N", Font = FONT, TextSize = 22, Color = C.White,
        Align = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, Parent = floatBtn,
    })
end

-- เอฟเฟกต์สายฟ้าดำรอบลูกลอย (ปิดได้โดยตั้ง FLOAT_LIGHTNING = false)
local FLOAT_LIGHTNING = true
if FLOAT_LIGHTNING then
    local F_GLOW = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150)
    local F_CORE = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)

    -- เงาดำรอบปุ่ม กะพริบเบา ๆ
    local fs = new("UIStroke", {
        Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0), Thickness = 3, Transparency = 0.3,
        ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border, Parent = floatBtn,
    })
    TweenService:Create(fs, TweenInfo.new(1.1, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1, true), {
        Transparency = 0.75,
    }):Play()

    local fLayer = new("Frame", {
        Name = "FloatLightning", BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 51, Parent = floatBtn,
    })

    local function fSeg(parent, a, b, thick, color, transp)
        local d = b - a
        local f = Instance.new("Frame")
        f.BorderSizePixel = 0
        f.BackgroundColor3 = color
        f.BackgroundTransparency = transp
        f.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        f.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(d.Magnitude + thick * 0.5, thick)
        local m = (a + b) / 2
        f.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(m.X, m.Y)
        f.Rotation = math.deg(math.atan2(d.Y, d.X))
        f.Parent = parent
        return f
    end

    local fEdges = {
        { Vector2.new(0, 0),         Vector2.new(1, 0) },
        { Vector2.new(FLOAT, 0),     Vector2.new(0, 1) },
        { Vector2.new(FLOAT, FLOAT), Vector2.new(-1, 0) },
        { Vector2.new(0, FLOAT),     Vector2.new(0, -1) },
    }

    local function fPath(parent, pts, tg, tc)
        for i = 1, #pts - 1 do
            fSeg(parent, pts[i], pts[i + 1], tg, F_GLOW, 0.55)
            fSeg(parent, pts[i], pts[i + 1], tc, F_CORE, 0)
        end
    end

    local function floatBolt()
        local e = fEdges[math.random(1, 4)]
        local start, dir = e[1], e[2]
        local normal = Vector2.new(dir.Y, -dir.X) -- ชี้ออกนอกปุ่ม
        local L = math.random(18, 34)
        local t0 = 10 + math.random() * (FLOAT - 20 - L) -- เลี่ยงมุมโค้ง
        local n = math.max(math.floor(L / 5), 4)

        local holder = new("Frame", {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 51, Parent = fLayer,
        })
        local pts = {}
        for i = 0, n do
            local off = (i == 0 or i == n) and 1 or (math.random() * 1.6 - 0.2) * 5 -- เอนออกนอกปุ่มเป็นหลัก
            pts[i + 1] = start + dir * (t0 + L * i / n) + normal * off
        end
        fPath(holder, pts, 4, 1.2)

        if math.random() < 0.6 then
            local k = math.random(2, n)
            local bdir = (normal + dir * (math.random() * 2 - 1) * 0.8).Unit
            local bp = { pts[k] }
            for i = 1, 3 do
                bp[i + 1] = bp[i] + bdir * 5 + Vector2.new(math.random() * 4 - 2, math.random() * 4 - 2)
            end
            fPath(holder, bp, 3, 1)
        end

        task.delay(0.06, function() if holder.Parent then holder.Visible = false end end)
        task.delay(0.10, function() if holder.Parent then holder.Visible = true end end)
        task.delay(0.20, function() holder:Destroy() end)
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        while ToggleGui.Parent do
            task.wait(0.1 + math.random() * 0.25)
            if ToggleGui.Parent then
                pcall(floatBolt)
                if math.random() < 0.4 then pcall(floatBolt) end
            end
        end
    end)
end

-- แตะ = เปิด/ปิดเมนู | ลาก = ย้ายลูกลอย
uiDraggable(floatBtn, floatBtn, toggleMainWindow)

    end

    -- เปิดให้สคริปต์ฟังก์ชันภายนอกใช้ระบบภาษาร่วมกันได้
    pcall(function()
        local env = (getgenv and getgenv()) or _G
        env.NAPHUB_UI = {
            Gui = ScreenGui,
            Tr = tr,                                   -- Tr(textLabel, "ไทย", "English")
            SetLang = function(l) setLang(l); langLbl.Text = LANG end,
            GetLang = function() return LANG end,      -- "TH" หรือ "EN"
            OnLang = function(cb) LANG_CALLBACKS[#LANG_CALLBACKS + 1] = cb end,
        }
    end)
end

-- Keep the original state-driven visibility option.
if State.FloatingButtonVisible == false then
    ToggleGui.Enabled = false
end

-- เปลี่ยนสีธีม (UI ล้วน)
local function applyThemePreset(name)
    local c = ThemePresets[name]
    if not c then return end
    Accent = c
    for _, hook in ipairs(AccentHooks) do
        pcall(hook)
    end
end
applyThemePreset(State.CurrentTheme)

local function noop() end

-- ====================================================================
-- ORIGINAL LOGOUT FUNCTION (logic preserved)
-- ====================================================================
local gui = ScreenGui
-- Logout :  /  key /  Start
local function performOriginalLogout()
    playClickSound()
    stopAllScriptOperations()
    performLogoutKeyClear()

    pcall(function()
        local pg = (typeof(gethui) == "function") and gethui() or CoreGui
        local tracerG = pg:FindFirstChild("ESP_Tracers")
        local fovG = pg:FindFirstChild("NAPHUBFOV")
        local notifH = pg:FindFirstChild("ObsidianGlass_NotifHolder")
        local lootG = pg:FindFirstChild("WarZ_LootESP")
        if tracerG then tracerG:Destroy() end
        if fovG then fovG:Destroy() end
        if notifH then notifH:Destroy() end
        if lootG then lootG:Destroy() end
    end)

    if gui and gui.Parent then
        gui:Destroy()
    end
    if ToggleGui and ToggleGui.Parent then
        ToggleGui:Destroy()
    end

    pcall(function()
        loadstring(game:HttpGet("https://raw.githubusercontent.com/aslamdunk7/paypmboygang/refs/heads/main/Start"))()
    end)
end

-- ====================================================================
-- ORIGINAL MAIN TAB / FEATURE CALLBACK BRIDGE
-- ====================================================================
local MainTab = Window:Tab({
    Title = "หน้าหลัก",
    Icon = "home"
})

MainTab:Section({
    Title = "⚔️ การต่อสู้และป้องกันตัว",
    Column = 1
})

local function createObsidianToggle(name, iconText, titleText, descText, defaultValue, callback)
    local control = MainTab:Toggle({
        Title = tostring(iconText or "") .. "  " .. tostring(titleText or "") ..
            "  •  " .. tostring(descText or ""),
        Value = defaultValue == true,
        Callback = function(state)
            if callback then
                callback(state)
            end
        end
    })
    return control
end
